394710* LEO EL DATASET PARA CARGARLO EN LA TABLA.                                   
394720******************************************************************

394740    PERFORM 90000005-B05INSTRUMEN-FINDN                                       
394750    IF WS-STATUS-BASE > 0                                                     
394760      IF WS-STATUS-BASE = 1                                                   
394770         MOVE 1 TO WS-7VEZ                                                    
394780      ELSE                                                                    
394810        STRING "ERROR AL HACER FIND A B05 ",   WS-STATUS-BASE                 
394820               DELIMITED BY SIZE INTO WS-0101-TEXT-MSG                        
394830        PERFORM 70000050-MENSAJE                                              
394840        CALL SYSTEM DMTERMINATE.                                              
394850                                                                              
394860    IF WS-7VEZ = 1                                                            
394870       NEXT SENTENCE                                                          
394880    ELSE                                                                      
394890       PERFORM 20000820-LLENO      THRU 20000820-EXIT.                        
394900 20000810-EXIT.                                                               
394910   EXIT.

394950******************************************************************

395030     CALL SYSTEM DMTERMINATE.                                                 
395040

395060   MOVE B05-NUM-INSTRUM                TO WS-408INS(WS-IND)                   
395070   MOVE B05-MONEDA                     TO WS-408MON(WS-IND)                   
395080   MOVE 0                              TO WS-408ABO(WS-IND)                   
395090   MOVE 0                              TO WS-408CAR(WS-IND)                   
395100   MOVE 0                              TO WS-408NUMA(WS-IND)                  
395110   MOVE 0                              TO WS-408NUMC(WS-IND).
